Pair line search details:
Given feasible h, current corr c_l for l=0..n-1. Choose i≠j. Let a=h[i], b=h[j], d=abs(i-j). Let δ. Variables: x=a+δ, y=b-δ. Bounds for δ:
L = max(-a, b-1)
U = min(1-a, b)
For each lag l:
- If l=0: q(δ)= (a+δ)(1-a-δ)+(b-δ)(1-b+δ) + rest0. Expand: rest0 = c0 - a(1-a)-b(1-b). = rest0 + (a+b) +? Let's compute exactly evaluate function in terms δ: x(1-x)+y(1-y). We can compute q0(δ) = (a+δ)*(1-a-δ) + (b-δ)*(1-b+δ) + rest0. This yields quadratic coefficient -2δ^2? Let's expand:
x-x^2 = a+δ - (a^2+2aδ+δ^2)=a-a^2 + δ(1-2a)-δ^2.
y-y^2 = b-δ - (b^2-2bδ+δ^2)=b-b^2 -δ(1-2b)-δ^2.
sum = const + δ(1-2a -1+2b) -2δ^2 = const + 2(b-a)δ -2δ^2. But rest0 excludes const; can compute direct for grid.
- For l>0: contributions from i and j to c_l. c_l = rest_l + terms involving i,j. General terms: each variable appears possibly as first factor with coefficient (1 - neighbor) and second with -neighbor. For pair, if i and j differ by l (or l? Since lag l pairs positions (m, m-l) with m=l..n-1). A term h_m(1-h_{m-l}) can involve:
   * i alone: if m=i (i≥l) or m-l=i (i+l<n)
   * j alone similarly
   * both: if {m,m-l} = {i,j} (distance l), term either x(1-y) or y(1-x) depending positions.
Since pair could share a product if abs(i-j)=l. For that lag, the term is quadratic if both changed.
We can compute the entire contribution to lag l for given δ by evaluating original formula? Since n=160, pair line search grid 64; for each candidate δ, we could compute new h for i,j and update full corr? Too slow if many pairs. But we can derive linear/quadratic coefficients per lag by scanning lags O(n) per pair. Then evaluate 64 δ via vectorized arrays of coefficients (constant, linear, quadratic). That is O(n) + O(G) per pair, good. For random 10k pairs => 1.6M lag operations + 640k eval, okay.

Let's derive pair update coefficients robustly by explicitly updating contribution:
For each lag l≥1, c_l = ∑_{m=l}^{n-1} h_m(1-h_{m-l}). We need express as A_l + B_l*δ + Q_l*δ^2.
Initialize A = c_l (current), B=0, Q=0? But if using old h_i, h_j, after change, contribution change Δ. We can compute Δ by summing changed terms. Easier: For each lag l, determine which terms in the sum involve i or j. There are at most 4 terms: term T_m for m such that m=i or m-l=i or m=j or m-l=j. If both variables in one term, need quadratic. Instead of classifying, we can compute new terms by iterating over m candidates set {i, i+l, j, j+l} if valid m in [l,n-1] and both endpoints within. Let's derive coefficients by considering each term h_m(1-h_{m-l}). Let u = h_m_old, v = h_{m-l}_old. Under pair (i,j), define mapping old to new: h_m_new = u + Δ_m, h_{m-l}_new = v + Δ_{m-l}, where Δ_p is δ if p=i, -δ if p=j, 0 otherwise.
Then term = (u+d_m)(1 - v - d_{m-l}) = u(1-v) + d_m(1-v) - d_{m-l} u - d_m d_{m-l}.
So Δ = d_m(1-v) - d_{m-l} u - d_m d_{m-l}.
Since d_p ∈ {δ, -δ, 0}. Let A1 = d_m constant? We can express Δ = (α)δ + (quadratic coeff)δ^2. Let d_m = s_m δ, d_{m-l}=s_{m-l} δ where s ∈ {1,-1,0}. Then Δ = δ[s_m(1-v) - s_{m-l}u] - s_m s_{m-l} δ^2.
If s_m and s_{m-l} not both nonzero, quadratic term zero (unless same? if m=m-l impossible l>0). If both nonzero and same term, -s_m s_{m-l} δ^2. If s_m=1 (m=i), s_{m-l}=-1 (m-l=j) => -1*(-1)=+1 δ^2. If m=j, m-l=i => -(-1*1)=+1 δ^2. If same variable? m=i and m-l=i impossible.
If a variable appears in multiple terms of same lag (for l small, i can be m and m-l for different m), contributions add linear terms. No term has same variable both? For l>0, a fixed p appears as first in m=p and second in m=p+l, distinct terms, linear add.

For each lag l, candidates are m in [l,n-1] that are i,j,i+l,j+l. That means l must be within distances:
- m=i: l≤i and l≤? m=i valid if l≤i and i≤n-1 true; m-l=i-l≥0. So l=1..i.
- m-l=i => m=i+l valid if i+l≤n-1 => l=1..n-1-i.
- similarly for j.
We can initialize B,Q zero and update ranges with vectorized slices? For a fixed pair, the possible affected l are union of [1,i], [1,n-1-i], [1,j], [1,n-1-j], and distance |i-j| for quadratic. This could be many (almost all). For each l in that union, need add contributions. Could loop over union length up to n-1. For n=160, fine. For each l, compute four possible terms. To avoid checking all m with conditions, derive contributions per l:
For variable p (i or j), as first factor m=p: if l≤p, add term with u=h[p], v=h[p-l], s_m=+1 if p==i else -1, s_prev=0 (unless p-l=other var? handled by generic), B += s_p*(1-v), Q +=0 except if p-l is other var => s_prev = +/-, Q += -s_p*s_prev.
As second factor m=p+l: if p+l≤n-1, u=h[p+l], v=h[p], s_prev = s_p, s_m=0: B += -s_p*u, Q += +? if p+l other var then s_m other, Q += -s_prev*s_m.
Thus for each p, first at p contributes for l≤p: s_p*(1 - h[p-l]). If p-l==q, Q += -s_p*s_q, B same with old h[q].
Second at p contributes for l≤n-1-p: -s_p*h[p+l]. If p+l==q, Q += -s_prev*s_m = -s_p*s_q.
So for ranges l=1..p and l=1..n-1-p, B gets sequences: h[p-l] and h[p+l]. We can vectorize B updates for ranges:

Pair update function:
def pair_attempt(h, c, i, j, grid_pts=65):
   n=len(h); a=h[i]; b=h[j]
   L = max(-a, b-1.0); U = min(1.0-a, b)
   if U - L <= 1e-12: return None
   B = np.zeros(n); Q = np.zeros(n)
   B[0]=2*(b-a); Q[0]=-2.0
   d = abs(i-j)
   if d>0 and d<n: Q[d]=1.0
   # vectorized B ranges
   # For p=i,s=1.0:
   p=i; s=1.0
   if p>0: B[1:p+1] += s*(1.0 - h[p-1:0:-1])
   m = n-1-p
   if m>0: B[1:m+1] += -s*h[p+1:p+m+1]
   p=j; s=-1.0
   if p>0: B[1:p+1] += s*(1.0 - h[p-1:0:-1])
   m = n-1-p
   if m>0: B[1:m+1] += -s*h[p+1:p+m+1]
   # Now c current array raw. A=c.
   # line search. Use unscaled raw max.
   M = max(c) (could pass M)
   # Generate δs. But B might be large; c + Bδ + Qδ^2.
   # We can narrow initial δ range: Since at δ=0 value M; if moving to bounds maybe. Use grid over L,U.
   # possible issue L,U large; optimum may be near 0. Use grid plus local.
   step = (U-L)
   # Use global grid: maybe include 0 always.
   ds = np.linspace(L, U, grid_pts)
   # ensure 0 included? 0 within. Not necessary but we can add.
   # To reduce allocation, if step small, grid enough.
   # Evaluate:
   # vals = np.max(c[None,:] + B[None,:]*ds[:,None] + Q[None,:]*(ds[:,None]**2), axis=1)
   # But if Q only few, computing Q matrix allocates. We can optimize using linear max plus special quadratics.
   lin = c[None,:] + B[None,:]*ds[:,None]
   vals = np.max(lin, axis=1)
   q0 = c[0] + B[0]*ds + Q[0]*ds*ds
   if d>0:
       qd = c[d] + B[d]*ds + Q[d]*ds*ds
       vals = np.maximum(vals, qd)
   vals = np.maximum(vals, q0)
   best = ds[np.argmin(vals)]
   best_val = min(vals)
   # If best_val >= M - 1e-12, maybe skip, but local refinement could find slightly lower? Global grid best near 0, if no improvement skip. If best_val < M - 1e-13:
       # local refine around best. Let lo=max(L,best - 2*step/grid), hi=min(U,best+2*step/grid) but if best at boundary, widen other side? Use radius = max(2*step/grid, 1e-4*step+1e-4). For numerical.
       # local grid 201.
   # after best, if improved, update h and c.
   h[i]=a+best; h[j]=b-best
   c = c + B*best + Q*best*best  # in-place? c_new = c + ...; return new array
   return best, c_new, B,Q? If updated, c_new.

B0=2(b-a), Q0=-2.

Now SLSQP functions top-level:
def smooth_soft_value_grad(x, n, beta):
    c = c_array_from_h(x) # uses correlate O(n^2). For SLSQP many evals, this is okay n=160.
    m = np.max(c)
    # for numerical, if beta large, exp underflow
    s = beta*(c-m)
    s = np.clip(s, -745, 0)
    e = np.exp(s)
    z = np.sum(e)
    val = m + np.log(z)/beta
    p = e/z
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    # c0
    grad += p[0]*(1.0 - 2.0*x)
    # positive lags
    # We can loop l=1..n-1
    # Potential p tiny; skip if <1e-12.
    for l in range(1,n):
       w=p[l]
       if w <= 1e-14: continue
       # j from l to n-1: coeff 1-h[j-l]
       grad[l:] += w*(1.0 - x[:n-l])
       # j from 0 to n-1-l: coeff -x[j+l]
       grad[:n-l] -= w*x[l:]
    return val, grad

Pair_pass implementation:
```
def pair_pass(h, c, pair_i, pair_j, rng, grid_pts=65, max_time=..., t0=..., budget_s=?):
    n=len(h)
    M=np.max(c)
    count=len(pair_i)
    order=rng.permutation(count)
    improved_in_pass=0
    accepted_in_pass=0
    for idx in order:
       if time.time()-t0 > budget_s - 30: break
       # pair_attempt
       res=pair_attempt(..., M)
       if res is not None:
           h,c,M=res; accepted_in_pass+=1
           improved_in_pass+=1
           if accepted_in_pass%200==0:
               c2=c_array_from_h(h); M2=c2.max(); # if similar, use corrected
               c=c2; M=M2
    return h,c,M,improved_in_pass
```

LP:
```
def bundle_direction(h,c,M,active_lags,eps):
    n=len(h)
    r=len(active_lags)
    Arows=[]
    for l in active_lags:
       g=zeros(n)
       ...
       Arows.append(np.append(g,-1.0))
    A=np.vstack(Arows)
    cobj=np.zeros(n+1); cobj[-1]=1
    Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1
    bounds=[(-eps,eps)]*n + [(-10.0,10.0)]
    res=linprog(cobj,A_ub=A,b_ub=np.zeros(r),A_eq=Aeq,b_eq=[0],bounds=bounds,method='highs')
    if res.success and res.fun < -1e-7:
       d=res.x[:n]
       # maybe normalize? res.fun ~ max g_l d
       return d, res.fun
    return None
```
But constraints g_l d - t ≤0 with t variable. If all g_l d positive, t positive; optimum t maybe positive. We want negative t. If t negative, exists d with all active g_l d negative. Since d=0 gives t=0, LP can choose t=0. It will find negative if possible. Good.
But due sum=0, direction may be d=0 if no descent. We can add a small regularization objective to avoid zero? no.

Line search evaluation function `eval_delta_values(c,B,Q,ds,d)` top-level:
```
def eval_delta_values(c,B,Q,ds,d):
    vals=np.max(c[None,:]+B[None,:]*ds[:,None],axis=1)
    q0=c[0]+B[0]*ds+Q[0]*ds*ds
    vals=np.maximum(vals,q0)
    if d>0:
       qd=c[d]+B[d]*ds+Q[d]*ds*ds
       vals=np.maximum(vals,qd)
    return vals
```
Because Q only 0 and d. For safety if any other Q nonzero? We only set those. Good.

Local refine:
```
def pair_attempt(...):
   ...
   ds = np.linspace(L,U,grid_pts)
   # ensure 0 included if interior
   if L < -1e-12 and U > 1e-12:
       if not np.any(np.abs(ds)< (U-L)/grid_pts/10): ds=np.insert(ds,int(len(ds)//2),0.0)
   vals=eval_delta_values(c,B,Q,ds,d)
   bi=int(np.argmin(vals)); bdiff=ds[bi]; bval=vals[bi]
   if bval < M - 1e-10:
       span=(U-L)/max(1,grid_pts-1)
       lo=max(L,bdiff-3*span); hi=min(U,bdiff+3*span)
       if hi-lo > 1e-12:
          # expand a bit
          lo=max(L,bdiff-5*span); hi=min(U,bdiff+5*span)

def get_pair_arrays(n, cache):
   if n not in cache:
      idx_i, idx_j = np.triu_indices(n,1)
      cache[n]=(idx_i, idx_j)
   return cache[n]
```

Let's implement `c_array_from_h` robust:
```
def c_array_from_h(h):
   n=len(h)
   full=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
   center=n-1
   c=np.empty(n, dtype=float)
   c[0]=float(full[center])
   if n>1: c[1:]=full[center+1:center+1+n-1]
   return c
```
`full[center+1:]` length n-1.

```
full=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); return float(np.max(full)* (2.0/len(h)))
```

def bundle_polish(h, c, c5, n, rng, t0, budget_s, reserve_s=40, max_attempts=12):
    global? no
    M = float(c5*n/2.0)? But easier pass M = max(c). We'll pass M.
    h_best=h.copy(); c_best=c.copy(); c5_best=c5; M_best=M
    for attempt in range(max_attempts):
       if time.time()-t0 > budget_s-reserve_s: break
       tol_base = max(0.25, 0.004*M_best)
       tol = tol_base * (1.0 + attempt%5)
       active = np.where(c_best >= M_best - tol)[0]
       if len(active)<2:
           active = np.argpartition(c_best, -5)[-5:]
       if len(active)>24:
           # top by c plus some random from active
           order=np.argsort(c_best)[::-1][:24]
           active=np.unique(np.concatenate((order, rng.choice(len(c_best), size=6, replace=False))))
       for eps in (0.02,0.05,0.1):
           if time... break
           d=bundle_direction(h_best, active, eps)
           if d is None: continue
           res=direction_line_search(h_best,d,M_best,grid=65,max_alpha=1.0)
           if res is not None:
               h_best,c_best,M_best=res
               c5_best=M_best*2/n
               if c5_best < M_best? update; break eps
    return h_best,c_best,c5_best,M_best
```

`direction_line_search` raw M:
```
def direction_line_search(h,d,M,grid_pts=73,max_alpha=1.0):
    n=len(h)
    lo=0.0; hi=0.0
    for idx in np.where(np.abs(d)>1e-15)[0]:
       dk=d[idx]; hk=h[idx]
       if dk>0:
          up=(1-hk)/dk
          dn=-(hk)/dk
          hi=min(hi,up); lo=min(lo,dn)
       else:
          up=hk/(-dk)
          dn=(1-hk)/dk
          hi=min(hi,up); lo=max(lo,dn)
    lo=min(lo,0.0); hi=max(hi,0.0)
    lo=max(lo,-max_alpha); hi=min(hi,max_alpha)
    if hi<=lo+1e-12: return None
    alphas=np.linspace(lo,hi,grid_pts)
    if not np.any(np.abs(alphas)<...): insert 0
    best_a=0.0; best_m=M
    # optional evaluate in chunks to reduce memory? build h each.
    for? Vectorizing h + αd for all α: H = h[None,:] + alphas[:,None]*d[None,:] shape (G,n); correlate each row? np.correlate doesn't batch. Loop G.
    for a in alphas:
       if a==0 continue
       cand=h + a*d
       # project? direction should bounds but floating; clip maybe and project? For line search exact, if slightly out, clip; but sum unchanged? Clipping changes sum. Since bounds computed, within.
       c=c_array_from_h(cand)
       m=max(c)
       if m<best_m: best_m=m; best_a=a
    if best_m < M - 1e-10:
       return h+best_a*d, c best? Need c array for best to avoid recompute. Store best_c.
    return None
```
Store best_c during loop. For α=0 c=current. Good. G 73*n=160 => 12k evaluations? Wait 73 correlations each O25k =1.8M per line search. 10 attempts 18M, ok.

Detailed functions:

`project_simplex(h, n)`:
```
def project_simplex(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n) # maybe resample better but not called mismatched
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    target = n/2.0
    for _ in range(50):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0: break
            add = diff / cnt
            h[mask] += add
            if add < 0: pass
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0: break
            h[mask] += diff / cnt
        np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    s=float(np.sum(h)); diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
        if diff >0:
            idx=np.argsort(1.0-h)[::-1]
            rem=diff
            for k in idx:
                if rem<=1e-14: break
                space=1.0-h[k]
                if space<=0: continue
                add=min(space, rem)
                h[k]+=add; rem-=add
        else:
            idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
            rem=-diff
            for k in idx:
                if rem<=1e-14: break
                rem_avail=h[k]
                if rem_avail<=0: continue
                sub=min(rem_avail, rem)
                h[k]-=sub; rem-=sub
    return h
```
The iterative equal add to all unsaturated may not project L2 but feasible. It could take many iterations if diff large and saturates; ≤n. For n up to 320, fine.
If diff large, add could exceed headroom for some; clip and recalc. Good.

`resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(h_src,n_src,n_dst):
    if n_src==n_dst:
       return project_simplex(h_src,n_dst)
    xp=(np.arange(n_src)+0.5)*(2.0/n_src)
    xn=(np.arange(n_dst)+0.5)*(2.0/n_dst)
    flat=0.5*h_src
    h_dst=np.interp(xn,xp,h_src)
    # maybe preserve total? project
    return project_simplex(h_dst,n_dst)
```
Use cell midpoints. Could blend with flat? no.

`initial_fallback`:
```
def initial_fallback(n, rng):
    t=np.arange(n)/float(n)
    h=np.full(n,0.5)
    for freq in (2,3,5,7):
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       amp=0.15/np.sqrt(float(freq))
       h += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*t + phase)
    h += 0.05*rng.randn(n)
    return project_simplex(h,n)
```
No closures.

`make_pair_arrays`:
```
def make_pair_arrays(n):
    if n <=1: return np.empty(0,dtype=int), np.empty(0,dtype=int)
    i,j=np.triu_indices(n,1)
    return i.astype(np.int64), j.astype(np.int64)
```
Could be memory; okay.

`eval_delta_values` as above. Ensure d < len(c). If d==0 skip qd.

`pair_attempt`:
Input `h,c,M,i,j,grid_pts,local_pts`. c array length n.
Need use original c if skip. We'll update h only on accept.
```
def pair_attempt(h,c,M,i,j,global_pts,local_pts):
    n=len(h)
    a=float(h[i]); b=float(h[j])
    L = max(-a, b-1.0)
    U = min(1.0-a, b)
    if U - L < 1e-12: return None
    B=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    Q=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    B[0]=2.0*(b-a)
    Q[0]=-2.0
    d=abs(i-j)
    if 0 < d < n:
       Q[d]=1.0
    # p i
    p=i; s=1.0
    if p>0:
       B[1:p+1] += (1.0 - h[p-1:0:-1])
    m=n-1-p
    if m>0:
       B[1:m+1] -= h[p+1:p+m+1]
    p=j; s=-1.0
    if p>0:
       B[1:p+1] -= (1.0 - h[p-1:0:-1])
    m=n-1-p
    if m>0:
       B[1:m+1] += h[p+1:p+m+1]
    # grid
    if global_pts < 5: global_pts=5
    ds=np.linspace(L,U,int(global_pts))
    # ensure 0 added
    if ds.size>1:
       if L < -1e-14 and U > 1e-14:
          zero=np.array([0.0])
          # insert if not close
          if np.min(np.abs(ds)) > (U-L)/(global_pts*10):
             ds=np.concatenate((ds, zero))
    vals=eval_delta_values(c,B,Q,ds,d)
    bi=int(np.argmin(vals)); bval=float(vals[bi]); bdelta=float(ds[bi])
    if bval < M - 1e-10:
       span=(U-L)/max(1,int(global_pts)-1)
       lo=max(L, bdelta - 4.0*span)
       hi=min(U, bdelta + 4.0*span)
       if hi - lo > 1e-10:
          lds=np.linspace(lo,hi,int(local_pts))
          lvals=eval_delta_values(c,B,Q,lds,d)
          li=int(np.argmin(lvals))
          lval=float(lvals[li])
          if lval < bval:
             bval=lval; bdelta=float(lds[li])
    if bval >= M - 1e-10:
       return None
    new_i=a+bdelta; new_j=b-bdelta
    # clamp tiny
    if new_i < 0: new_i=0.0
    elif new_i > 1: new_i=1.0
    if new_j < 0: new_j=0.0
    elif new_j > 1: new_j=1.0
    # If clamp changes pair sum? adjust delta? If clamp needed, recompute? Shouldn't.
    if abs((new_i+new_j)-(a+b)) > 1e-12:
       # fallback: recompute safe new values with projection of pair? use original b-delta but maybe clamp
       # Adjust new_j = a+b-new_i
       new_j = a+b-new_i
       if new_j<0 or new_j>1: return None
    h[i]=new_i; h[j]=new_j
    c_new = c + B*bdelta + Q*bdelta*bdelta
    M_new = float(np.max(c_new))
    if M_new >= M - 1e-9:
       # revert
       h[i]=a; h[j]=b
       return None
    return h, c_new, M_new
```
Potential Q B arrays: For p=i second slice `B[1:m+1] -= h[p+1:p+m+1]`; for p=j second `+= h[...]` because s=-1. Good.
For p=i first: coefficient s=1: + (1-h). p=j first: s=-1: - (1-h). Good.

But what about if i or j at boundary and slices overlap? B additions cumulative. Fine.

`eval_delta_values` with `c[1:]` ignores c0 linear. But if n=1? n>=2. If c[1:] length n-1. `lin = c[1:][None,:] + B[1:][None,:]*ds[:,None]`. `np.max` if n=1? n-1=0; but n>1. For n=1 not.
```
def eval_delta_values(c,B,Q,ds,d):
    lin = c[1:][None,:] + B[1:][None,:]*ds[:,None]
    vals = np.max(lin, axis=1)
    q0 = c[0] + B[0]*ds + Q[0]*ds*ds
    vals = np.maximum(vals, q0)
    if d > 0 and d < len(c):
       qd = c[d] + B[d]*ds + Q[d]*ds*ds
       vals = np.maximum(vals, qd)
    return vals
```
If n=2, c[1:] length1. OK.

`pair_pass` as above. Need `pair_i` arrays maybe empty. If count=0 return.
```
def pair_pass(h,c,pair_i,pair_j,rng,global_pts,local_pts,deadline,t0,budget_s):
    n=len(h); M=float(np.max(c)); count=len(pair_i); improved=0
    if count==0: return h,c,M,0,True
    order=rng.permutation(count)
    completed=True
    for pos in range(count):
        if time.time() > deadline or time.time()-t0 > budget_s-30:
            completed=False; break
        if pos % 500 ==0? no
        res=pair_attempt(h,c,M,int(pair_i[pos]),int(pair_j[pos]),global_pts,local_pts)
        if res is not None:
            h,c,M=res
            improved +=1
            if improved % 200 ==0:
                c2=c_array_from_h(h); M2=float(np.max(c2))
                # if drift significant or just correct
                if abs(M2-M)>1e-7 or True:
                   c=c2; M=M2
    return h,c,M,improved,completed
```
If c recomputed, pair_attempt coefficients for next pair use h and c. Good.
Using `True` recompute every 200 accepted; if no accepted, no. If many accepted, okay. If only few improved, no recompute.
Potential `pair_attempt` may return M_new based on c_new but if c_new inconsistent due to drift, recompute fixed. Good.

`bundle_direction`:
```
def bundle_direction(h, active, eps):
    n=len(h)
    active=np.asarray(active, dtype=int)
    active=active[(active>=0)&(active<n)]
    r=len(active)
    if r==0: return None
    Arows=[]
    for l in active:
       g=np.zeros(n)
       if l==0:
          g=1.0-2.0*h
       else:
          if l < n:
             g[l:] += 1.0 - h[:n-l]
             g[:n-l] -= h[l:]
       Arows.append(np.concatenate((g, np.array([-1.0]))))
    A=np.vstack(Arows)
    c=np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1.0
    Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1.0
    bounds=[(-eps, eps)]*n + [(-10.0,10.0)]
    try:
       res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(r), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=np.zeros(1), bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    except Exception:
       return None
    if not res.success: return None
    if res.fun >= -1e-7: return None
    d=res.x[:n]
    if np.max(np.abs(d)) < 1e-8: return None
    return d
```
Note constraints: For each row g_l d - t <=0. A row [g, -1]. b=0. Good.
Objective min t. If t negative, d descent. But if active set small, LP may find t very negative with d saturating. Good.
`linprog` with bounds list of tuples length n+1; for n=320, okay.
Could be slow if highsm not available; fallback? If method='highs' fails, try default? We can:
```
try: res=linprog(..., method='highs')
except: res=linprog(...)
```
But top-level. Use try.

`direction_line_search`:
```
def direction_line_search(h,d,M,grid_pts=73,max_alpha=1.0):
    n=len(h)
    lo=0.0; hi=0.0
    moved=np.nonzero(np.abs(d)>1e-14)[0]
    for k in moved:
       dk=float(d[k]); hk=float(h[k])
       if dk>0:
          hi=min(hi,(1.0-hk)/dk)
          lo=min(lo,-hk/dk)
       else:
          hi=min(hi,hk/(-dk))
          lo=max(lo,(1.0-hk)/dk)
    lo=min(lo,0.0); hi=max(hi,0.0)
    lo=max(lo,-max_alpha); hi=min(hi,max_alpha)
    if hi <= lo + 1e-12: return None
    alphas=np.linspace(lo,hi,int(grid_pts))
    if alphas.size>1 and lo < -1e-14 < hi:
       if np.min(np.abs(alphas)) > (hi-lo)/(grid_pts*10):
          alphas=np.concatenate((alphas,np.array([0.0])))
    best_a=0.0; best_m=M; best_c=None
    # current c maybe not passed; to store best_c if alpha 0 skip. We'll recompute best c at end if a!=0.
    for a in alphas:
       if abs(a) < 1e-15: continue
       cand=h + a*d
       # Due to numerical, clip if tiny out
       if np.min(cand) < -1e-12 or np.max(cand) > 1.0+1e-12:
          # if significantly out, skip
          if np.min(cand) < -1e-8 or np.max(cand) > 1.0+1e-8:
             continue
          np.clip(cand,0.0,1.0,out=cand)
          cand=project_simplex(cand,n) # too expensive per alpha? avoid; just clip? sum changes tiny.
       c=c_array_from_h(cand)
       m=float(np.max(c))
       if m < best_m - 1e-11:
          best_m=m; best_a=a; best_c=c.copy()
    if best_c is None: return None
    # If best_a not 0, ensure h updated
    h_new = h + best_a*d
    np.clip(h_new,0.0,1.0,out=h_new)
    # project if sum off
    if abs(float(np.sum(h_new)) - n/2.0) > 1e-9:
       # adjust best_c? recompute
       h_new=project_simplex(h_new,n)
       c_new=c_array_from_h(h_new)
       m_new=float(np.max(c_new))
       if m_new >= M - 1e-11: return None
       best_c=c_new; best_m=m_new
    return h_new,best_c,best_m
```
Project per alpha expensive; only if out. Bounds should prevent.
But if we project after, c_new might not correspond to line; okay.

Could instead precompute feasible alphas with array operations for speed:
```
pos=d>0; if pos any: hi0=(1-h[pos]).min()/d[pos].max? Need elementwise, easier loop.
```
n small.

`bundle_polish`:
```
def bundle_polish(h,c,M,c5,n,rng,t0,budget_s,reserve_s,max_attempts):
    h_best=h.copy(); c_best=c.copy(); M_best=float(M); c5_best=float(c5)
    for attempt in range(max_attempts):
       if time.time()-t0 > budget_s-reserve_s: break
       tol_base=max(0.2, 0.003*M_best)
       tol=tol_base*(1.0 + 0.75*(attempt % 5))
       if attempt % 2 == 0:
          active=np.where(c_best >= M_best - tol)[0]
          if len(active)<3:
             active=np.argsort(c_best)[::-1][:8]
          if len(active)>24:
             top=np.argsort(c_best)[::-1][:18]
             extra=rng.choice(n, size=6, replace=False)
             active=np.unique(np.concatenate((top,extra)))
       else:
          active=rng.choice(n, size=min(12,n), replace=False)
       for eps in (0.02,0.05,0.1):
          if time.time()-t0 > budget_s-reserve_s: break
          d=bundle_direction(h_best, active, eps)
          if d is None: continue

    return h_best,c_best,M_best,c5_best

```
def smooth_value_grad(x,n,beta):
    c=c_array_from_h(x)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    s=beta*(c-m)
    s=np.clip(s,-700.0,0.0)
    e=np.exp(s)
    z=float(np.sum(e))
    if z <=0: z=1.0
    val=m + np.log(z)/beta
    p=e/z
    grad=np.zeros(n,float)
    if p[0] > 1e-14:
       grad += p[0]*(1.0-2.0*x)
    for l in range(1,n):
       w=p[l]
       if w <=1e-13: continue
       grad[l:] += w*(1.0 - x[:n-l])
       grad[:n-l] -= w*x[l:]
    return val, grad
```

Implement `binary_round`:
```
def binary_round(h,n,thresh):
   target=n//2
   b=(h>=thresh).astype(float)
   cnt=b.sum()
   if cnt<target:
      zeros=np.where(b==0)[0]
      if len(zeros)>target-cnt:
         # select top h among zeros
         order=np.argsort(h[zeros])[::-1][:target-cnt]
         b[zeros[order]]=1.0
   elif cnt>target:
      ones=np.where(b==1)[0]
      order=np.argsort(h[ones])[:cnt-target]
      b[ones[order]]=0.0
   return b
```
Need if cnt exactly. Since target int. Feasible. Good.

```
def c5_exact(h):
    n=len(h)
    full=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(full) * (2.0/n))
```

    h_best=project_simplex(h,n)
    c_best=c_array_from_h(h_best)
    best_M=float(np.max(c_best)); best_c5=best_M*(2.0/n)

Let's write `bundle_polish` active selection more robust:
```
def bundle_polish(h,c,M,c5,n,rng,stop_time,max_attempts):
    h_best=h.copy(); c_best=c.copy(); M_best=float(M); c5_best=float(M*2/n)
    for attempt in range(int(max_attempts)):
       if time.time()>stop_time: break
       tol_base=max(0.25, 0.003*M_best)
       tol=tol_base*(1.0 + 0.5*float(attempt % 5))
       if attempt %2==0:
          active=np.where(c_best >= M_best - tol)[0]
          if len(active)<4:
             active=np.argsort(c_best)[::-1][:min(8,n)]
          if len(active)>24:
             top=np.argsort(c_best)[::-1][:18]
             extra=np.random? Use rng.choice(n, size=6, replace=False)
             # if n<6 adjust
             if n>=6:
                 extra=rng.choice(n, size=6, replace=False)
             else:
                 extra=rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=False)
             active=np.unique(np.concatenate((top,extra)))
       else:
          size=min(12,n)
          active=rng.choice(n, size=size, replace=False)
       for eps_val in (0.02,0.05,0.1):
          if time.time()>stop_time: break
          d=bundle_direction(h_best, active, eps_val)
          if d is None: continue
          res=direction_line_search(h_best,d,M_best,grid_pts=81,max_alpha=1.0)
          if res is not None:
             h_best,c_best,M_best=res
             c5_best=M_best*(2.0/n)
             break
    return h_best,c_best,M_best,c5_best
```

def direction_line_search(h,d,M,grid_pts=81,max_alpha=1.0):

Define `search_alphas(h,d,alphas,M)` to avoid duplicate. It loops alphas, returns (a,m,c). c None if a=0 no improvement? But we need for local compare. It can return c for best if best_a !=0 else None.
```
def search_alphas(h,d,alphas,M):
    best_a=0.0; best_m=float(M); best_c=None
    for a in alphas:
       if abs(a)<1e-14: continue
       cand=h+a*d
       # bounds check
       c=c_array_from_h(cand)
       m=float(np.max(c))
       if m < best_m - 1e-12:
          best_m=m; best_a=a; best_c=c.copy()
    return best_a,best_m,best_c
```

Now `binary_round`: target int. If h has sum target but threshold count not, adjust. Ensure final sum target by exact: target = n//2 if even. If n even. If n not even? We'll only even. But function can handle target=n/2.
```
def binary_round(h,n,thresh):
    target=n//2
    b=(h >= thresh).astype(float)
    cnt=int(np.sum(b))
    if cnt < target:
       zeros=np.nonzero(b==0.0)[0]
       need=target-cnt
       if need>0 and len(zeros)>=need:
          order=np.argsort(h[zeros])[::-1][:need]
          b[zeros[order]]=1.0
    elif cnt > target:
       ones=np.nonzero(b==1.0)[0]
       need=cnt-target
       if need>0 and len(ones)>=need:
          order=np.argsort(h[ones])[:need]
          b[ones[order]]=0.0
    # ensure sum exact maybe adjust if not
    s=int(np.sum(b)); diff=target-s
    if diff>0: zeros=... set top h to 1
    elif diff<0: ones=... set smallest h to 0
    return b
```

Full code draft:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

def project_simplex(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n)
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = n / 2.0
    for _ in range(60):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            h[mask] += diff / cnt
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            h[mask] += diff / cnt
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    s = float(np.sum(h))
    diff = target - s
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(diff, max(0.0, 1.0 - h[idx]))
            h[idx] += add
            diff -= add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            sub = min(-diff, max(0.0, h[idx]))
            h[idx] -= sub
            diff += sub
    return h
```
Good.

`c_array_from_h`:
```
def c_array_from_h(h):
    n = len(h)
    full = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    center = n - 1
    c = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    c[0] = float(full[center])
    c[1:] = full[center + 1: center + n]
    return c
```
`center+n` = 2n-1? center+1 length n-1; slicing center+1:center+n (excl) length n-1. Good.

`c5_exact` okay.

`make_pair_arrays`:
```
def make_pair_arrays(n):
    if n <= 1:
       return np.empty(0,dtype=np.int64), np.empty(0,dtype=np.int64)
    a,b=np.triu_indices(n,1)
    return a.astype(np.int64), b.astype(np.int64)
```

`eval_delta_values` okay.

`pair_attempt` full:
```
def pair_attempt(h, c, M, i, j, global_pts, local_pts):
    n=len(h)
    a=float(h[i]); b=float(h[j])
    L=max(-a, b-1.0)
    U=min(1.0-a, b)
    if U - L < 1e-12: return None
    B=np.zeros(n, dtype=float); Q=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    B[0]=2.0*(b-a); Q[0]=-2.0
    d=abs(i-j)
    if 0 < d < n: Q[d]=1.0
    p=i
    if p>0: B[1:p+1] += (1.0 - h[p-1:0:-1])
    m=n-1-p
    if m>0: B[1:m+1] -= h[p+1:p+m+1]
    p=j
    if p>0: B[1:p+1] -= (1.0 - h[p-1:0:-1])
    m=n-1-p
    if m>0: B[1:m+1] += h[p+1:p+m+1]
    gp=int(global_pts)
    if gp < 5: gp=5
    ds=np.linspace(L,U,gp)
    if ds.size > 1 and L < -1e-14 and U > 1e-14:
       if float(np.min(np.abs(ds))) > (U-L)/(gp*10.0):
           ds=np.concatenate((ds, np.array([0.0])))
    vals=eval_delta_values(c,B,Q,ds,d)
    bi=int(np.argmin(vals)); bval=float(vals[bi]); bdelta=float(ds[bi])
    if bval < M - 1e-10:
       span=(U-L)/max(1,gp-1)
       lo=max(L, bdelta-4.0*span); hi=min(U, bdelta+4.0*span)
       if hi - lo > 1e-11:
           lp=int(local_pts)
           if lp < 31: lp=31
           lds=np.linspace(lo,hi,lp)
           lvals=eval_delta_values(c,B,Q,lds,d)
           li=int(np.argmin(lvals)); lval=float(lvals[li])
           if lval < bval:
               bval=lval; bdelta=float(lds[li])
    if bval >= M - 1e-10:
       return None
    new_i=a+bdelta; new_j=b-bdelta
    if new_i < 0.0: new_i=0.0
    elif new_i > 1.0: new_i=1.0
    if new_j < 0.0: new_j=0.0
    elif new_j > 1.0: new_j=1.0
    if abs((new_i+new_j)-(a+b)) > 1e-11:
       new_j=a+b-new_i
       if new_j < 0.0 or new_j > 1.0:
           return None
    h[i]=new_i; h[j]=new_j
    c_new = c + B*bdelta + Q*bdelta*bdelta
    M_new = float(np.max(c_new))
    if M_new >= M - 1e-9:
       h[i]=a; h[j]=b
       return None
    return h, c_new, M_new
```
If span computed with gp after maybe ds length gp+1 if inserted; okay. local radius 4*span. If global optimum at boundary and best at boundary, lo/hi extends inward. Good.

`pair_pass`:
```
def pair_pass(h,c,pair_i,pair_j,rng,global_pts,local_pts,deadline,stop_time):
    n=len(h); M=float(np.max(c)); count=len(pair_i); improved=0
    if count ==0:
       return h,c,M,0,True
    order = rng.permutation(count)
    completed=True
    for pos in range(count):
       if time.time() > deadline or time.time() > stop_time:
          completed=False; break
       res=pair_attempt(h,c,M,int(pair_i[pos]),int(pair_j[pos]),global_pts,local_pts)
       if res is not None:
          h,c,M=res
          improved +=1
          if improved % 250 ==0:
             c2=c_array_from_h(h)
             M2=float(np.max(c2))
             c=c2; M=M2
    return h,c,M,improved,completed
```
If deadline less than now initially, completed false. okay.

`search_alphas`:
```
def search_alphas(h,d,alphas,M):
    best_a=0.0; best_m=float(M); best_c=None
    for a in alphas:
       af=float(a)
       if abs(af) < 1e-14: continue
       cand = h + af*d
       mn = float(np.min(cand)); mx = float(np.max(cand))
       if mn < -1e-8 or mx > 1.0 + 1e-8:
          continue
       if mn < 0.0 or mx > 1.0:
          np.clip(cand,0.0,1.0,out=cand)
       c = c_array_from_h(cand)
       m = float(np.max(c))
       if m < best_m - 1e-11:
          best_m=m; best_a=af; best_c=c.copy()
    return best_a,best_m,best_c
```
If multiple alphas, okay. If best_a remains 0 best_c None.

`direction_line_search`:
```
def direction_line_search(h,d,M,grid_pts=81,max_alpha=1.0):
    n=len(h)
    lo=0.0; hi=0.0
    moved=np.nonzero(np.abs(d)>1e-14)[0]
    for k in moved:
       dk=float(d[k]); hk=float(h[k])
       if dk > 0.0:
          hi=min(hi, (1.0-hk)/dk)
          lo=min(lo, -hk/dk)
       else:
          hi=min(hi, hk/(-dk))
          lo=max(lo, (1.0-hk)/dk)
    lo=min(lo,0.0); hi=max(hi,0.0)
    lo=max(lo,-max_alpha); hi=min(hi,max_alpha)
    if hi <= lo + 1e-13:
       return None
    gp=int(grid_pts)
    if gp<21: gp=21
    alphas=np.linspace(lo,hi,gp)
    if alphas.size>1 and lo < -1e-14 and hi > 1e-14:
       if float(np.min(np.abs(alphas))) > (hi-lo)/(gp*10.0):
           alphas=np.concatenate((alphas, np.array([0.0])))
    best_a,best_m,best_c=search_alphas(h,d,alphas,M)
    if best_c is not None:
       step=(hi-lo)/gp
       lo2=max(lo, best_a-4.0*step); hi2=min(hi, best_a+4.0*step)
       if hi2 - lo2 > 1e-12:
          lalphas=np.linspace(lo2,hi2,121)
          la,lm,lc=search_alphas(h,d,lalphas,M)
          if lc is not None and lm < best_m - 1e-12:
             best_a,best_m,best_c=la,lm,lc
    if best_c is None:
       return None
    h_new = h + best_a*d
    if abs(float(np.sum(h_new)) - n/2.0) > 1e-9:
       h_new = project_simplex(h_new, n)
       c_new = c_array_from_h(h_new)
       m_new=float(np.max(c_new))
       if m_new >= M - 1e-11:
          # if projection ruined, try clipped original?
          h_new = h + best_a*d
          np.clip(h_new,0.0,1.0,out=h_new)
          c_new = best_c
          m_new = best_m
    else:
       c_new = best_c
       m_new = best_m
    return h_new, c_new, m_new
```

if m_new >= M - 1e-10: return None
return h_new,c_new,m_new
```

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        p = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        n0 = int(len(p))
    except Exception:
        p = None
        n0 = 160
    if p is None or n0 < 32:
        n0 = 160
        p = initial_fallback(n0, rng)
    if n0 % 2 == 1:
        n_even = (n0 + 1) // 2 * 2
        if n_even < 64:
            n_even = 160
        p = resample_h(p, n0, n_even)
        n0 = n_even
    else:
        p = project_simplex(p, n0)
    best_h = p.copy()
    best_n = n0
    best_c = c_array_from_h(best_h)
    best_M = float(np.max(best_c))
    best_c5 = best_M * (2.0 / float(best_n))
    try:
        ev = float(evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h))
        if ev < best_c5:
            best_c5 = ev
    except Exception:
        pass

    res_order = [best_n]
    for m in (best_n + 32, 192, 256, best_n + 16, 320, 224):
        if m % 2 == 0 and 80 <= m <= 320:
            if m not in res_order:
                res_order.append(m)
    if len(res_order) > 5:
        res_order = res_order[:5]
    # ensure 256 maybe not if truncated? For 160 order [160,192,256,176,320] yes. For 192 [192,224,256,208,320].

    work_h = {}
    work_c5 = {}
    pair_cache = {}
    visit_count = {}
    current = 0
    hard_stop = t0 + total_budget

    def? no nested. Use main_stop = t0 + max(60.0, total_budget - 330.0)
    main_stop = t0 + max(60.0, total_budget - 330.0)
    while time.time() < main_stop:
        n_res = res_order[current % len(res_order)]
        v = visit_count.get(n_res, 0)
        if v == 0:
            phase_limit = 240.0
        elif v == 1:
            phase_limit = 120.0
        elif v == 2:
            phase_limit = 60.0
        else:
            phase_limit = 30.0
        rem = main_stop - time.time()
        if rem < 25.0:
            break
        if phase_limit > rem:
            phase_limit = rem
        if n_res in work_h and work_c5.get(n_res, 1e9) <= best_c5 + 0.002:
            h0 = work_h[n_res].copy()
        else:
            if n_res == best_n:
                h0 = best_h.copy()
            else:
                h0 = resample_h(best_h, best_n, n_res)
        c0 = c_array_from_h(h0)
        M0 = float(np.max(c0))
        c5_0 = M0 * (2.0 / float(n_res))
        if c5_0 < best_c5 + 1e-5:
            c5_check = c5_exact(h0)
            if c5_check < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c5_check
                best_h = h0.copy()
                best_n = n_res
        phase_start = time.time()
        phase_deadline = min(main_stop, phase_start + phase_limit)
        if n_res not in pair_cache:
            pair_cache[n_res] = make_pair_arrays(n_res)
        pi, pj = pair_cache[n_res]
        if n_res <= 200:
            gp = 81
            lp = 171
        elif n_res <= 280:
            gp = 65
            lp = 141
        else:
            gp = 57
            lp = 121
        stop_time = t0 + total_budget - 35.0
        while time.time() < phase_deadline:
            chunk_deadline = min(phase_deadline, time.time() + 45.0)
            if chunk_deadline - time.time() < 5.0:
                chunk_deadline = phase_deadline
            h0, c0, M0, imp, comp = pair_pass(h0, c0, pi, pj, rng, gp, lp, chunk_deadline, stop_time)
            c5_0 = M0 * (2.0 / float(n_res))
            if c5_0 < best_c5 + 1e-5:
                c5_check = c5_exact(h0)
                if c5_check < best_c5:
                    best_c5 = c5_check
                    best_h = h0.copy()
                    best_n = n_res
            if imp == 0:
                if comp:
                    break
                if time.time() - phase_start > 25.0:
                    break
        work_h[n_res] = h0
        work_c5[n_res] = M0 * (2.0 / float(n_res))
        visit_count[n_res] = v + 1
        current += 1
        if current > 3 * len(res_order) and best_c5 < 0.3806:
            # maybe continue? We can break? no, still can improve. Don't break.
            pass
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog


def project_simplex(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n)
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = n / 2.0

    for _ in range(60):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            h[mask] += diff / cnt
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            h[mask] += diff / cnt
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    s = float(np.sum(h))
    diff = target - s
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(diff, max(0.0, 1.0 - h[idx]))
            h[idx] += add
            diff -= add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            sub = min(-diff, max(0.0, h[idx]))
            h[idx] -= sub
            diff += sub
    return h


def c_array_from_h(h):
    n = len(h)
    full = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    center = n - 1
    c = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    c[0] = float(full[center])
    if n > 1:
        c[1:] = full[center + 1: center + n]
    return c


def c5_exact(h):
    n = len(h)
    full = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(full) * (2.0 / float(n)))


def make_pair_arrays(n):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64), np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    a, b = np.triu_indices(n, 1)
    return a.astype(np.int64), b.astype(np.int64)


def eval_delta_values(c, B, Q, ds, d):
    lin = c[1:][None, :] + B[1:][None, :] * ds[:, None]
    vals = np.max(lin, axis=1)
    q0 = c[0] + B[0] * ds + Q[0] * ds * ds
    vals = np.maximum(vals, q0)
    if d > 0 and d < len(c):
        qd = c[d] + B[d] * ds + Q[d] * ds * ds
        vals = np.maximum(vals, qd)
    return vals


def pair_attempt(h, c, M, i, j, global_pts, local_pts):
    n = len(h)
    a = float(h[i])
    b = float(h[j])

    L = max(-a, b - 1.0)
    U = min(1.0 - a, b)
    if U - L < 1e-12:
        return None

    B = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    Q = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    B[0] = 2.0 * (b - a)
    Q[0] = -2.0

    d = abs(i - j)
    if 0 < d < n:
        Q[d] = 1.0

    p = i
    if p > 0:
        B[1:p + 1] += (1.0 - h[p - 1:0:-1])
    m = n - 1 - p
    if m > 0:
        B[1:m + 1] -= h[p + 1:p + m + 1]

    p = j
    if p > 0:
        B[1:p + 1] -= (1.0 - h[p - 1:0:-1])
    m = n - 1 - p
    if m > 0:
        B[1:m + 1] += h[p + 1:p + m + 1]

    gp = int(global_pts)
    if gp < 5:
        gp = 5
    ds = np.linspace(L, U, gp)
    if ds.size > 1 and L < -1e-14 and U > 1e-14:
        if float(np.min(np.abs(ds))) > (U - L) / (gp * 10.0):
            ds = np.concatenate((ds, np.array([0.0])))

    vals = eval_delta_values(c, B, Q, ds, d)
    bi = int(np.argmin(vals))
    bval = float(vals[bi])
    bdelta = float(ds[bi])

    if bval < M - 1e-10:
        span = (U - L) / max(1, gp - 1)
        lo = max(L, bdelta - 4.0 * span)
        hi = min(U, bdelta + 4.0 * span)
        if hi - lo > 1e-11:
            lp = int(local_pts)
            if lp < 31:
                lp = 31
            lds = np.linspace(lo, hi, lp)
            lvals = eval_delta_values(c, B, Q, lds, d)
            li = int(np.argmin(lvals))
            lval = float(lvals[li])
            if lval < bval:
                bval = lval
                bdelta = float(lds[li])

    if bval >= M - 1e-10:
        return None

    new_i = a + bdelta
    new_j = b - bdelta

    if new_i < 0.0:
        new_i = 0.0
    elif new_i > 1.0:
        new_i = 1.0
    if new_j < 0.0:
        new_j = 0.0
    elif new_j > 1.0:
        new_j = 1.0

    if abs((new_i + new_j) - (a + b)) > 1e-11:
        new_j = a + b - new_i
        if new_j < 0.0 or new_j > 1.0:
            return None

    h[i] = new_i
    h[j] = new_j
    c_new = c + B * bdelta + Q * bdelta * bdelta
    M_new = float(np.max(c_new))

    if M_new >= M - 1e-9:
        h[i] = a
        h[j] = b
        return None

    return h, c_new, M_new


def pair_pass(h, c, pair_i, pair_j, rng, global_pts, local_pts, deadline, stop_time):
    n = len(h)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    count = len(pair_i)
    improved = 0
    if count == 0:
        return h, c, M, 0, True

    order = rng.permutation(count)
    completed = True

    for pos in range(count):
        if time.time() > deadline or time.time() > stop_time:
            completed = False
            break

        res = pair_attempt(h, c, M, int(pair_i[pos]), int(pair_j[pos]), global_pts, local_pts)
        if res is not None:
            h, c, M = res
            improved += 1
            if improved % 250 == 0:
                c2 = c_array_from_h(h)
                M2 = float(np.max(c2))
                c = c2
                M = M2

    return h, c, M, improved, completed


def bundle_direction(h, active, eps):
    n = len(h)
    active = np.asarray(active, dtype=int)
    active = active[(active >= 0) & (active < n)]
    r = len(active)
    if r == 0:
        return None

    rows = []
    for l in active:
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        if l == 0:
            g = 1.0 - 2.0 * h
        else:
            if l < n:
                g[l:] += 1.0 - h[:n - l]
                g[:n - l] -= h[l:]
        rows.append(np.concatenate((g, np.array([-1.0], dtype=float))))

    A = np.vstack(rows)
    cobj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    cobj[-1] = 1.0

    Aeq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=float)
    Aeq[0, :n] = 1.0

    bounds = [(-float(eps), float(eps))] * n + [(-10.0, 10.0)]

    try:
        res = linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(r), A_eq=Aeq,
                      b_eq=np.zeros(1), bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.zeros(r), A_eq=Aeq,
                          b_eq=np.zeros(1), bounds=bounds)
        except Exception:
            return None

    if not res.success:
        return None
    if res.fun >= -1e-7:
        return None

    d = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
    d -= float(np.sum(d)) / float(n)
    nm = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if nm < 1e-8:
        return None
    if nm > eps * 1.001:
        d *= (float(eps) / nm)
    return d


def search_alphas(h, d, alphas, M):
    best_a = 0.0
    best_m = float(M)
    best_c = None

    for a in alphas:
        af = float(a)
        if abs(af) < 1e-14:
            continue

        cand = h + af * d
        mn = float(np.min(cand))
        mx = float(np.max(cand))
        if mn < -1e-8 or mx > 1.0 + 1e-8:
            continue
        if mn < 0.0 or mx > 1.0:
            np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1.0, out=cand)

        c = c_array_from_h(cand)
        m = float(np.max(c))
        if m < best_m - 1e-11:
            best_m = m
            best_a = af
            best_c = c.copy()

    return best_a, best_m, best_c


def direction_line_search(h, d, M, grid_pts=81, max_alpha=1.0):
    n = len(h)
    lo = 0.0
    hi = 0.0

    moved = np.nonzero(np.abs(d) > 1e-14)[0]
    for k in moved:
        dk = float(d[k])
        hk = float(h[k])
        if dk > 0.0:
            hi = min(hi, (1.0 - hk) / dk)
            lo = min(lo, -hk / dk)
        else:
            hi = min(hi, hk / (-dk))
            lo = max(lo, (1.0 - hk) / dk)

    lo = min(lo, 0.0)
    hi = max(hi, 0.0)
    lo = max(lo, -float(max_alpha))
    hi = min(hi, float(max_alpha))

    if hi <= lo + 1e-13:
        return None

    gp = int(grid_pts)
    if gp < 21:
        gp = 21
    alphas = np.linspace(lo, hi, gp)
    if alphas.size > 1 and lo < -1e-14 and hi > 1e-14:
        if float(np.min(np.abs(alphas))) > (hi - lo) / (gp * 10.0):
            alphas = np.concatenate((alphas, np.array([0.0])))

    best_a, best_m, best_c = search_alphas(h, d, alphas, M)

    if best_c is not None:
        step = (hi - lo) / gp
        lo2 = max(lo, best_a - 4.0 * step)
        hi2 = min(hi, best_a + 4.0 * step)
        if hi2 - lo2 > 1e-12:
            lalphas = np.linspace(lo2, hi2, 121)
            la, lm, lc = search_alphas(h, d, lalphas, M)
            if lc is not None and lm < best_m - 1e-12:
                best_a, best_m, best_c = la, lm, lc

    if best_c is None:
        return None

    h_new = h + best_a * d
    np.clip(h_new, 0.0, 1.0, out=h_new)
    if abs(float(np.sum(h_new)) - n / 2.0) > 1e-8:
        h_new = project_simplex(h_new, n)

    c_new = c_array_from_h(h_new)
    m_new = float(np.max(c_new))
    if m_new >= M - 1e-10:
        return None

    return h_new, c_new, m_new


def bundle_polish(h, c, M, c5, n, rng, stop_time, max_attempts):
    h_best = h.copy()
    c_best = c.copy()
    M_best = float(M)
    c5_best = float(M * 2.0 / float(n))

    for attempt in range(int(max_attempts)):
        if time.time() > stop_time:
            break

        tol_base = max(0.25, 0.003 * M_best)
        tol = tol_base * (1.0 + 0.5 * float(attempt % 5))

        if attempt % 2 == 0:
            active = np.where(c_best >= M_best - tol)[0]
            if len(active) < 4:
                active = np.argsort(c_best)[::-1][:min(8, n)]
            if len(active) > 24:
                top = np.argsort(c_best)[::-1][:18]
                if n >= 6:
                    extra = rng.choice(n, size=6, replace=False)
                else:
                    extra = rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=False)
                active = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, extra)))
        else:
            size = min(12, n)
            active = rng.choice(n, size=size, replace=False)

        for eps_val in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1):
            if time.time() > stop_time:
                break
            d = bundle_direction(h_best, active, eps_val)
            if d is None:
                continue
            res = direction_line_search(h_best, d, M_best, grid_pts=81, max_alpha=1.0)
            if res is not None:
                h_best, c_best, M_best = res
                c5_best = M_best * (2.0 / float(n))
                break

    return h_best, c_best, M_best, c5_best


def smooth_value_grad(x, n, beta):
    c = c_array_from_h(x)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    s = beta * (c - m)
    s = np.clip(s, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(s)
    z = float(np.sum(e))
    if z <= 0.0:
        z = 1.0
    val = m + np.log(z) / beta
    p = e / z

    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if p[0] > 1e-14:
        grad += p[0] * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)

    for l in range(1, n):
        w = p[l]
        if w <= 1e-13:
            continue
        grad[l:] += w * (1.0 - x[:n - l])
        grad[:n - l] -= w * x[l:]

    return val, grad


def smooth_fun(x, n, beta):
    return float(smooth_value_grad(x, n, beta)[0])


def smooth_jac(x, n, beta):
    return smooth_value_grad(x, n, beta)[1]


def sum_eq(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - n / 2.0)


def sum_eq_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones_like(x, dtype=float)


def slsqp_polish(h, n, stop_time):
    h_best = project_simplex(h, n)
    c_best = c_array_from_h(h_best)
    best_M = float(np.max(c_best))
    best_c5 = best_M * (2.0 / float(n))

    h_cur = h_best.copy()
    if time.time() > stop_time - 5.0:
        return h_best, best_M, best_c5

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': sum_eq, 'jac': sum_eq_jac, 'args': (n,)}]

    betas = (80.0,)
    for beta in betas:
        if time.time() > stop_time - 5.0:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_fun, h_cur, method='SLSQP', jac=smooth_jac,
                args=(n, float(beta)), bounds=bounds, constraints=cons,
                options={'maxiter': 8, 'ftol': 1e-9, 'iprint': 0}
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        cand = project_simplex(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float), n)
        c = c_array_from_h(cand)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        c5 = M * (2.0 / float(n))

        if c5 < best_c5 - 1e-11:
            best_M = M
            best_c5 = c5
            h_best = cand.copy()

        h_cur = cand.copy()

    return h_best, best_M, best_c5


def resample_h(h_src, n_src, n_dst):
    if n_src == n_dst:
        return project_simplex(h_src, n_dst)
    xp = (np.arange(n_src) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_src))
    xn = (np.arange(n_dst) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_dst))
    h_dst = np.interp(xn, xp, h_src)
    return project_simplex(h_dst, n_dst)


def initial_fallback(n, rng):
    t = np.arange(n) / float(n)
    h = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
    for freq in (2, 3, 5, 7):
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        amp = 0.15 / np.sqrt(float(freq))
        h += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * t + phase)
    h += 0.05 * rng.randn(n)
    return project_simplex(h, n)


def binary_round(h, n, thresh):
    target = n // 2
    b = (h >= float(thresh)).astype(float)

    cnt = int(np.sum(b))
    if cnt < target:
        zeros = np.nonzero(b == 0.0)[0]
        need = target - cnt
        if need > 0 and len(zeros) >= need:
            order = np.argsort(h[zeros])[::-1][:need]
            b[zeros[order]] = 1.0
    elif cnt > target:
        ones = np.nonzero(b == 1.0)[0]
        need = cnt - target
        if need > 0 and len(ones) >= need:
            order = np.argsort(h[ones])[:need]
            b[ones[order]] = 0.0

    s = int(np.sum(b))
    diff = target - s
    if diff > 0:
        zeros = np.nonzero(b == 0.0)[0]
        if len(zeros) >= diff:
            order = np.argsort(h[zeros])[::-1][:diff]
            b[zeros[order]] = 1.0
    elif diff < 0:
        ones = np.nonzero(b == 1.0)[0]
        if len(ones) >= -diff:
            order = np.argsort(h[ones])[: -diff]
            b[ones[order]] = 0.0

    return b


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        p = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        n0 = int(len(p))
    except Exception:
        p = None
        n0 = 160

    if p is None or n0 < 32:
        n0 = 160
        p = initial_fallback(n0, rng)

    if n0 % 2 == 1:
        n_even = (n0 + 1) // 2 * 2
        if n_even < 64:
            n_even = 160
        p = resample_h(p, n0, n_even)
        n0 = n_even
    else:
        p = project_simplex(p, n0)

    best_h = p.copy()
    best_n = n0
    best_c = c_array_from_h(best_h)
    best_M = float(np.max(best_c))
    best_c5 = best_M * (2.0 / float(best_n))

    try:
        ev = float(evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h))
        if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < best_c5:
            best_c5 = ev
    except Exception:
        pass

    res_order = [best_n]
    for m in (best_n + 32, 192, 256, best_n + 16, 320, 224):
        if m % 2 == 0 and 80 <= m <= 320 and m not in res_order:
            res_order.append(m)
    if len(res_order) > 5:
        res_order = res_order[:5]

    work_h = {}
    work_c5 = {}
    pair_cache = {}
    visit_count = {}
    current = 0

    main_stop = t0 + max(60.0, total_budget - 330.0)
    hard_stop = t0 + total_budget

    while time.time() < main_stop:
        n_res = res_order[current % len(res_order)]
        v = visit_count.get(n_res, 0)

        if v == 0:
            phase_limit = 240.0
        elif v == 1:
            phase_limit = 120.0
        elif v == 2:
            phase_limit = 60.0
        else:
            phase_limit = 30.0

        rem = main_stop - time.time()
        if rem < 25.0:
            break
        if phase_limit > rem:
            phase_limit = rem

        if n_res in work_h and work_c5.get(n_res, 1e9) <= best_c5 + 0.002:
            h0 = work_h[n_res].copy()
        else:
            if n_res == best_n:
                h0 = best_h.copy()
            else:
                h0 = resample_h(best_h, best_n, n_res)

        c0 = c_array_from_h(h0)
        M0 = float(np.max(c0))
        c5_0 = M0 * (2.0 / float(n_res))

        if c5_0 < best_c5 + 1e-5:
            c5_check = c5_exact(h0)
            if c5_check < best_c5:
                best_c5 = c5_check
                best_h = h0.copy()
                best_n = n_res

        phase_start = time.time()
        phase_deadline = min(main_stop, phase_start + phase_limit)

        if n_res not in pair_cache:
            pair_cache[n_res] = make_pair_arrays(n_res)
        pi, pj = pair_cache[n_res]

        if n_res <= 200:
            gp = 81
            lp = 171
        elif n_res <= 280:
            gp = 65
            lp = 141
        else:
            gp = 57
            lp = 121

        stop_time = t0 + total_budget - 35.0

        while time.time() < phase_deadline:
            chunk_deadline = min(phase_deadline, time.time() + 45.0)
            if chunk_deadline - time.time() < 5.0:
                chunk_deadline = phase_deadline

            h0, c0, M0, imp, comp = pair_pass(
                h0, c0, pi, pj, rng, gp, lp, chunk_deadline, stop_time
            )
            c5_0 = M0 * (2.0 / float(n_res))

            if c5_0 < best_c5 + 1e-5:
                c5_check = c5_exact(h0)
                if c5_check < best_c5:
                    best_c5 = c5_check
                    best_h = h0.copy()
                    best_n = n_res

            if imp == 0:
                if comp:
                    break
                if time.time() - phase_start > 25.0:
                    break

        work_h[n_res] = h0
        work_c5[n_res] = M0 * (2.0 / float(n_res))
        visit_count[n_res] = v + 1
        current += 1

    if time.time() < hard_stop - 130.0:
        c_best = c_array_from_h(best_h)
        M_best = float(np.max(c_best))
        c5_best = M_best * (2.0 / float(best_n))
        best_h, c_best, M_best, c5_best = bundle_polish(
            best_h, c_best, M_best, c5_best, best_n, rng,
            t0 + total_budget - 130.0, 8
        )
        c5_check = c5_exact(best_h)
        if c5_check < best_c5:
            best_c5 = c5_check
            best_n = len(best_h)

    perturb_stop = t0 + total_budget - 70.0
    if time.time() < perturb_stop:
        nS = best_n
        if nS not in pair_cache:
            pair_cache[nS] = make_pair_arrays(nS)
        pi, pj = pair_cache[nS]

        if nS <= 200:
            gp = 81
            lp = 171
        elif nS <= 280:
            gp = 65
            lp = 141
        else:
            gp = 57
            lp = 121

        sigmas = (0.004, 0.008, 0.016, 0.032, 0.064)
        for idx in range(len(sigmas)):
            if time.time() > perturb_stop:
                break
            sig = sigmas[idx]
            h2 = project_simplex(best_h + sig * rng.randn(nS), nS)
            c2 = c_array_from_h(h2)
            M2 = float(np.max(c2))
            c5_2 = M2 * (2.0 / float(nS))

            if c5_2 < best_c5 + 1e-5:
                c5_check = c5_exact(h2)
                if c5_check < best_c5:
                    best_c5 = c5_check
                    best_h = h2.copy()
                    best_n = nS
                    c2 = c_array_from_h(best_h)
                    M2 = float(np.max(c2))
                    c5_2 = M2 * (2.0 / float(nS))

            if c5_2 > best_c5 + 0.004:
                continue

            refine_stop = min(perturb_stop, t0 + total_budget - 40.0)
            if time.time() < refine_stop - 10.0:
                chunk_deadline = min(refine_stop, time.time() + 35.0)
                h2, c2, M2, imp, comp = pair_pass(
                    h2, c2, pi, pj, rng, gp, lp, chunk_deadline, refine_stop
                )
                c5_2 = M2 * (2.0 / float(nS))
                if c5_2 < best_c5 + 1e-5:
                    c5_check = c5_exact(h2)
                    if c5_check < best_c5:
                        best_c5 = c5_check
                        best_h = h2.copy()
                        best_n = nS

            if time.time() < perturb_stop - 25.0:
                c2 = c_array_from_h(h2)
                M2 = float(np.max(c2))
                h2, c2, M2, c5_3 = bundle_polish(
                    h2, c2, M2, M2 * 2.0 / float(nS), nS, rng,
                    min(perturb_stop, t0 + total_budget - 25.0), 3
                )
                c5_check = c5_exact(h2)
                if c5_check < best_c5:
                    best_c5 = c5_check
                    best_h = h2.copy()
                    best_n = nS

    binary_stop = t0 + total_budget - 60.0
    if time.time() < binary_stop:
        nS = best_n
        if nS not in pair_cache:
            pair_cache[nS] = make_pair_arrays(nS)
        pi, pj = pair_cache[nS]

        if nS <= 200:
            gp = 81
            lp = 171
        elif nS <= 280:
            gp = 65
            lp = 141
        else:
            gp = 57
            lp = 121

        for th in (0.45, 0.50, 0.55):
            if time.time() > binary_stop:
                break
            hb = binary_round(best_h, nS, th)
            cb = c_array_from_h(hb)
            Mb = float(np.max(cb))
            c5b = Mb * (2.0 / float(nS))

            if c5b < best_c5 + 1e-5:
                c5_check = c5_exact(hb)
                if c5_check < best_c5:
                    best_c5 = c5_check
                    best_h = hb.copy()
                    best_n = nS

            elif c5b < best_c5 + 0.002 and time.time() < binary_stop - 10.0:
                chunk_deadline = min(binary_stop, time.time() + 15.0)
                hb, cb, Mb, imp, comp = pair_pass(
                    hb, cb, pi, pj, rng, gp, lp, chunk_deadline, binary_stop
                )
                c5b = Mb * (2.0 / float(nS))
                if c5b < best_c5 + 1e-5:
                    c5_check = c5_exact(hb)
                    if c5_check < best_c5:
                        best_c5 = c5_check
                        best_h = hb.copy()
                        best_n = nS

    final_h = best_h.copy()
    final_n = best_n
    c_final = c_array_from_h(final_h)
    M_final = float(np.max(c_final))
    c5_final = M_final * (2.0 / float(final_n))
    c5_check = c5_exact(final_h)
    if c5_check < c5_final:
        c5_final = c5_check

    if final_n not in pair_cache:
        pair_cache[final_n] = make_pair_arrays(final_n)
    pi, pj = pair_cache[final_n]

    if final_n <= 200:
        gp = 81
        lp = 171
    elif final_n <= 280:
        gp = 65
        lp = 141
    else:
        gp = 57
        lp = 121

    final_pair_stop = t0 + total_budget - 20.0
    if time.time() < final_pair_stop:
        while time.time() < final_pair_stop:
            chunk_deadline = min(final_pair_stop, time.time() + 20.0)
            final_h, c_final, M_final, imp, comp = pair_pass(
                final_h, c_final, pi, pj, rng, gp, lp, chunk_deadline, final_pair_stop
            )
            c5_tmp = M_final * (2.0 / float(final_n))
            if c5_tmp < c5_final - 1e-12:
                c5_check = c5_exact(final_h)
                if c5_check < c5_final:
                    c5_final = c5_check
            if imp == 0 and comp:
                break

    if time.time() < t0 + total_budget - 12.0:
        c_final = c_array_from_h(final_h)
        M_final = float(np.max(c_final))
        final_h, c_final, M_final, c5_tmp = bundle_polish(
            final_h, c_final, M_final, M_final * 2.0 / float(final_n),
            final_n, rng, min(t0 + total_budget - 8.0, time.time() + 20.0), 3
        )
        c5_check = c5_exact(final_h)
        if c5_check < c5_final:
            c5_final = c5_check

    if time.time() < t0 + total_budget - 90.0 and final_n <= 220:
        h_s, M_s, c5_s = slsqp_polish(final_h, final_n, t0 + total_budget - 25.0)
        c5_check = c5_exact(h_s)
        if c5_check < c5_final:
            final_h = h_s
            c5_final = c5_check
            if time.time() < t0 + total_budget - 15.0:
                chunk_deadline = t0 + total_budget - 10.0
                final_h, c_final, M_final, imp, comp = pair_pass(
                    final_h, c_array_from_h(final_h), pi, pj, rng, gp, lp,
                    chunk_deadline, t0 + total_budget - 10.0
                )
                c5_check = c5_exact(final_h)
                if c5_check < c5_final:
                    c5_final = c5_check

    final_h = project_simplex(final_h, final_n)
    c_final = c_array_from_h(final_h)
    c5_final = float(np.max(c_final)) * (2.0 / float(final_n))

    try:
        ev = float(evaluate_erdos_solution(final_h))
        if np.isfinite(ev):
            c5_final = ev
    except Exception:
        pass

    return (final_h, c5_final, int(final_n))
```
